            }
            maps.insert(name.clone(),map);
        }
    }
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version'",[super::table_renames::VERSION])?;
    Ok(maps)
}
fn exists_table(conn:&Connection,name:&str)->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name=?1)",[name],|r|r.get(0))?)}
fn asset(kind:&crate::schema::ColumnType)->bool {use crate::schema::ColumnType::*;match kind{Optional(k)|List{of:k,..}|ListRange{of:k,..}=>asset(k),File{..}|Image{..}|Video{..}|Ref{..}=>true,_=>false}}

fn reference(kind:&crate::schema::ColumnType)->bool{use crate::schema::ColumnType::*;match kind{Optional(k)|List{of:k,..}|ListRange{of:k,..}=>reference(k),Ref{..}=>true,_=>false}}
//! Compare final-existence obligations in declared row/column identity space.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — spelling-only changes must not trigger a view.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use snapback4_core::schema::{Constraint, RuleTerm, Schema};
use crate::parser;

#[derive(Default)]
struct Identities {
    tables: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    columns: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
}
impl Identities {
    fn table(&self, name: &str) -> String {self.tables.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())}
    fn column(&self, table: &str, name: &str) -> String {
        self.columns.get(table).and_then(|c|c.get(name)).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||name.into())
    }
    fn term(&self, source: &str, term: &RuleTerm) -> RuleTerm {
        match term {
            RuleTerm::Field(c)=>RuleTerm::Field(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::OldField(c)=>RuleTerm::OldField(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>RuleTerm::NextField(self.column(source,c)),
            RuleTerm::Reference{table,column,field,old}=>RuleTerm::Reference{
                table:self.table(table),column:self.column(source,column),field:self.column(table,field),old:*old},
            RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,hops,old}=>RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{
                column:self.column(source,column),old:*old,hops:hops.iter().map(|h|snapback4_core::schema::RuleHop{
                    table:self.table(&h.table),field:self.column(&h.table,&h.field)}).collect()},
            RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)=>term.clone(),
        }
    }
    fn signature(&self,schema:&Schema,source:&str,constraint:&Constraint)->Option<(String,Vec<String>,Vec<RuleTerm>)> {
        let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=constraint else{return None};
        let index=schema.table(table)?.index(index)?;
        if !index.is_plain(){return None;}
        Some((self.table(table),index.columns().iter().map(|c|self.column(table,c)).collect(),
            key.iter().map(|t|self.term(source,t)).collect()))
    }
}

pub(super) fn relaxed(target:&[(String,String)],current:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    let old=target.iter().flat_map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if !old.iter().any(|t|!t.final_exists.is_empty()){return false;}
    let next=current.iter().flat_map(|(f,t)|parser::parse_document(f,t).0.tables).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let (before,after)=(crate::compile(target),crate::compile(current));
    if !before.diagnostics.is_empty()||!after.diagnostics.is_empty(){return true;}
    let mut identities=Identities::default();
    let mut pairs=Vec::new();
    for prior in &old {
        let Some(now)=next.iter().find(|n|n.name==prior.name||n.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&prior.name)||prior.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&n.name))else{continue};
        identities.tables.insert(now.name.clone(),prior.name.clone());
        let mut columns=BTreeMap::new();
        for c in &prior.columns {
            if let Some(n)=now.columns.iter().find(|n|n.name==c.name||n.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&c.name)||c.renamed_from.as_ref()==Some(&n.name)) {
                columns.insert(n.name.clone(),c.name.clone());
            }
        }
        if now.columns.iter().map(|c|columns.get(&c.name).unwrap_or(&c.name)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=now.columns.len(){return true;}
        identities.columns.insert(now.name.clone(),columns);
        pairs.push((prior.name.as_str(),now.name.as_str()));
    }
    // Never let two independent current identities collapse to one old name.
    if next.iter().map(|t|identities.table(&t.name)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len()!=next.len(){return true;}
    let unchanged=Identities::default();
    for (prior,now) in pairs {
        let (Some(old_table),Some(new_table))=(before.schema.table(prior),after.schema.table(now))else{return true};
        for constraint in &old_table.constraints {
            if !matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{..}){continue;}
            let Some(expected)=unchanged.signature(&before.schema,prior,constraint)else{return true};
            if !new_table.constraints.iter().any(|c|identities.signature(&after.schema,now,c).as_ref()==Some(&expected)){return true;}
        }
    }
    false
}
        for expr in [&mut column.read,&mut column.default].into_iter().flatten(){crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
        if let Some(expr)=column.maintained.as_mut().and_then(|a|a.filter.as_mut()){crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
    }
    for (expr,_) in &mut decl.final_exists {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
    // Sync targets are structural table probes outside the expression AST.
    if let Some(sync)=&decl.sync {
        let (tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,&source);
        // Lexer continuation rules keep indented `or` arms in this logical
        // declaration even when their physical line differs from `sync`.
        if let Some(start)=tokens.iter().position(|t|t.site==sync.site) {
            let end=tokens[start..].iter().position(|t|matches!(t.kind,Kind::Newline|Kind::Eof)).map_or(tokens.len(),|n|start+n);
            for pair in tokens[start..end].windows(2) {if pair[0].kind==Kind::Word && pair[1].value=="[" {if let Some(name)=aliases.get(&pair[0].value){let start=offset(&source,pair[0].site.line,pair[0].site.col);edits.push((start,start+pair[0].value.len(),name.clone()));}}}
        }
    }
    edits.sort();edits.dedup();
    if edits.iter().any(|(a,b,_)|a>b||*b>source.len()||!source.is_char_boundary(*a)||!source.is_char_boundary(*b))
        || edits.windows(2).any(|pair|pair[0].1>pair[1].0) {
        return Err("unsupported or overlapping retained-table source spans".into());
    }
    edits.sort_by_key(|e|std::cmp::Reverse(e.0));
    for (start,end,value) in edits {source.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
    Ok(source)
}
fn join(old:&ast::ScalarType,new:&ast::ScalarType,tables:&BTreeMap<String,(&String,&String,ast::TableDecl)>)->Option<ast::ScalarType> {
    let kind=match (&old.kind,&new.kind) {
        (TypeKind::Int|TypeKind::IntRange{..},TypeKind::Int|TypeKind::IntRange{..})=>{
            let bounds=|k:&TypeKind|match k{TypeKind::IntRange{min,max}=>(min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN),max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX)),_=>(i64::MIN,i64::MAX)};
            let(a,b)=bounds(&old.kind);let(x,y)=bounds(&new.kind);let(min,max)=(a.min(x),b.max(y));
            if min==i64::MIN && max==i64::MAX {TypeKind::Int}else{TypeKind::IntRange{min:(min!=i64::MIN).then_some(min),max:(max!=i64::MAX).then_some(max)}}
        }
        (TypeKind::Image{max:a,once:b},TypeKind::Image{max:x,once:y}) if b==y=>TypeKind::Image{max:(*a).max(*x),once:*b},
        (TypeKind::Video{max:a},TypeKind::Video{max:b})=>TypeKind::Video{max:(*a).max(*b)},
        (TypeKind::File{max:a,types:b},TypeKind::File{max:x,types:y}) if b==y=>TypeKind::File{max:(*a).max(*x),types:b.clone()},
        (TypeKind::Text{min:a,max:b,format:c},TypeKind::Text{min:x,max:y,format:z}) if c==z=>TypeKind::Text{min:Some(a.unwrap_or(0).min(x.unwrap_or(0))).filter(|v|*v>0),max:(*b).max(*y),format:c.clone()},
        (TypeKind::Enum{variants:a},TypeKind::Enum{variants:b})=>{let mut v=a.clone();for x in b {if !v.contains(x){v.push(x.clone());}}TypeKind::Enum{variants:v}},
        (TypeKind::List{item:a,min:b,max:c},TypeKind::List{item:x,min:y,max:z})=>TypeKind::List{item:Box::new(join(a,x,tables)?),min:(*b).min(*y),max:(*c).max(*z)},
        // Only an explicit rename provides nominal identity; structural similarity
        // or independently declared replacement tables cannot justify a bridge.
        (TypeKind::Ref{table:a},TypeKind::Ref{table:b}) if a==b || tables.get(a).is_some_and(|(_,_,t)| t.name==*b)=>TypeKind::Ref{table:a.clone()},
        (a,b) if a==b=>a.clone(),
        // Other conversions require an explicit typed migration. A bridge never
        // guesses a nominal reference, money currency or asset policy.
        _=>return None,
    };
    Some(ast::ScalarType{kind,optional:old.optional||new.optional})
}

#[test]
fn current_first_worker_dependency_cannot_disappear_from_an_intent_view() {
    let app=App::new();

    app.source(&format!("{}invariant docs.read only true\n",old.replace("amount: int 0..10","amount: int 0..20")));
    fs::write(app.0.join("snapback/worker.mjs"),"export {};\n").unwrap();
    for check in [true,false] {
        let args=if check {vec!["deploy","deployment","--check"]}else{vec!["deploy","deployment"]};
        let out=app.run(&args);assert!(!out.status.success(),"{}",text(&out));
        assert!(text(&out).contains("authored worker bundle"),"{}",text(&out));
        assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);

#[test]
fn equivalent_unique_labels_preserve_final_existence_in_default_rollback() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table items:\n  code: text <=20\n  n: int\n  unique byCode: code\n  public\n  online only\ntable records:\n  value: text <=20\n  public\n  after insert <- exists items[.value]\n  online only\nquery legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code] by byCode\n";
    app.source(old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":"a"})]);
    let current=old.replace("n: int", "n: int ?").replace("unique byCode:","unique byCurrentCode:")
        .replace("query legacyLookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code] by byCode\n", "");
    app.source(&current);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
}

#[test]
fn equivalent_unique_index_rename_keeps_default_rollback_visible() {
    let app=App::new();
    let old="table items:\n  n: int\n  code: text <=20\n  unique byCode: code\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery lookup(code: text <=20):\n  return items[code]\n";
    app.source(old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.seed(old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":"a"})]);
    let current=old.replace("n: int", "n: int ?").replace("unique byCode:","unique byCurrentCode:")
        .replace("table retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\n", "");
    app.source(&current);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
    assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
    app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
}

#[test]
fn scalar_renamed_unique_keys_keep_their_identity_in_default_rollback() {
    for (kind, value) in [("text <=20", json!("alice")), ("int", json!(7))] {
        for strengthen in [false,true] {
        let app=App::new();
        let old=format!("table items:\n  n: int\n  code: {kind}\n  memo: text <=20 ?\n  by byN: n, id\n  public\n  online only\ntable retired:\n  value: int\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items first 10 by byN\n");
        let old=if strengthen {old.replace("  by byN: n, id", "  by byN: n, id\n  by byCustomer: code")} else {old};
        app.source(&old);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
        app.seed(&old,vec![json!({"id":"kept","n":1,"code":value,"memo":null})]);
        let next=format!("table items:\n  n: int ?\n  label: {kind} renamed from code\n  count: int default 7\n  by byCount: count, label, id\n  unique byCustomer: label\n  public\n  online only\nquery ordered():\n  return items where .count = 7 first 10 by byCount\n");
        app.source(&next);
        app.ok(&["deploy","deployment","--check"]);app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
        let preview=app.json(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1","--check","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(preview["visibility"]["hiddenRows"],0);
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","1"]);
        let data=app.json(&["sql","SELECT code FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(data["rows"][0][0],value);
        assert_eq!(app.count(),1);
        app.ok(&["rollback","deployment","--to","latest"]);
        let forward=app.json(&["sql","SELECT label FROM items","--release","deployment","--json"]);
        assert_eq!(forward["rows"][0][0],value);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn unchanged_quarry_cannot_roll_back_an_executable_only_policy_repair() {
    let app=App::new();
    app.source("table docs:\n  owner: principal\n  public\n  online only\ninvariant docs.read only true\n");
    let worker=app.0.join("snapback/worker.mjs");fs::write(&worker,"export {}; // original\n").unwrap();
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]);
    app.ok(&["deploy","deployment"]); // Identical executable remains a no-op.
    assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);
    fs::write(&worker,"export {}; // policy repair with identical Quarry\n").unwrap();
    for check in [true,false] {
        let args=if check {vec!["deploy","deployment","--check"]}else{vec!["deploy","deployment"]};
        let out=app.run(&args);assert!(!out.status.success(),"{}",text(&out));
        assert!(text(&out).contains("authored worker bundle"),"{}",text(&out));
        assert_eq!(app.json(&["releases","deployment"])["current"],1);
    }
}
impl App {
    fn new() -> Self {
        static NEXT: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
        let p = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "sb4-roll-{}-{}",
            std::process::id(),
            NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)
        ));
        fs::create_dir_all(p.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        Self(p)
    }
    fn source(&self, s: &str) {
        fs::write(self.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), s).unwrap();
    }
    fn run(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        Command::new(BIN)
            .args(args)
            .current_dir(&self.0)
            .env_remove("PORT_LO")
            .env_remove("PORT_HI")
            .output()
            .unwrap()
    }
    fn ok(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        let o = self.run(args);
        assert!(o.status.success(), "{}", text(&o));
        o
    }
    fn json(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Value {
        let o = self.ok(args);
        serde_json::from_slice(&o.stdout).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{e}: {}", text(&o)))
    }
    fn seed(&self, source: &str, rows: Vec<Value>) {
        let b = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
        let store =
            snapback4_core::store_port::AnyStore::open(&self.0.join("deployment/store.sqlite"))
                .unwrap();
        let n = store.release_value().unwrap().unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        let schema = b.schema.clone();
        let mut e = snapback4_core::Engine::new_release(
            store,
            b.schema,
            b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program),
            n,
            Some(n),
        )
        .unwrap();
        let rows=rows.into_iter().map(|mut row| {
            if row.get("photos")==Some(&json!("fixture-image")) {
                let png=b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n\x00\x00\x00\x0dIHDR\x00\x00\x00\x01\x00\x00\x00\x01";
                let asset=e.store_asset("alice",png,1000).unwrap();
                row["photos"]=json!([snapback4_core::wire::to_json(&asset.column_value())]);
            }
            (
                "items".into(),
                snapback4_core::wire::row_from_json(&schema.tables["items"], &row).unwrap(),
            )
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        e.seed(rows)
        .unwrap();
    }
    fn count(&self) -> u64 {
        self.json(&[
            "sql",
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM items",
            "--release",
            "deployment",
            "--json",
        ])["rows"][0][0]
            .as_u64()
            .unwrap()
    }
}
impl Drop for App {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
    }
}
fn text(o: &Output) -> String {
    format!(
        "{}\n{}",
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout),
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr)
    )
}

fn newer() -> String {
    OLD.replace("amount: int 100..50000","amount: int 100..50000 ?").replace("  return insert payments","  require a.amount != null else NO_AMOUNT 'Amount to follow must not become a zero payment.'\n  return insert payments")
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct IndexDecl {
    pub name: String,
    /// The columns, in order, the `words(..)` component left out.
    pub columns: Vec<String>,
    pub unique: bool,
    pub refusal: Option<(String, Option<String>)>,
    /// `words(a, b)`: its position among the components (the number of
    /// columns before it) and the text columns it tokenizes. An index with
    /// one serves `matching` reads whose keys are exactly those columns,
    /// and nothing else.
    pub words: Option<(usize, Vec<String>)>,
    pub computed: std::collections::BTreeMap<String, Expr>,
    pub site: Site,
}

impl IndexDecl {
    /// Whether the index serves ordinary prefix reads and probes.
    pub fn is_plain(&self) -> bool {
        self.words.is_none() && self.computed.is_empty()
    }

    /// Whether a `matching` read with this many keys reads this index.
    pub fn searches_after(&self, keys: usize) -> bool {
        self.words.as_ref().is_some_and(|(position, _)| *position == keys)
    }
}

    Read,
    Insert,
    Update,

/// `sync public | to .column | to table[.a, .b] [by .principal] [last N by index]`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SyncDecl {
    pub audience: SyncAudience,
    pub horizon: Option<(u32, SyncOrder)>,
    pub own_keys: Option<String>,
    pub site: Site,

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.1 — column intent resolves to the same ordinary index as a named window.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SyncOrder {
    Index(String),
    Columns(Vec<String>),
}

impl std::fmt::Display for SyncOrder {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Index(name) => f.write_str(name),
            Self::Columns(columns) => f.write_str(&columns.iter().map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SyncAudience {
    Inferred { principal: Option<String> },
    Public,
    Column(String),
    Target { table: String, prefix: Vec<String>, principal: Option<String> },
    /// `sync to .owner or members[.team]`: column and target arms, as written.
    Union(Vec<SyncAudience>),
}

/// Independent policy intent; never used to grant runtime authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#2-surface — intent is separate from enforcement.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct InvariantDecl {
    pub table: String,
    pub target: String,
    pub column: Option<String>,
    pub value: Expr,
    pub site: Site,
}

impl TableDecl {
    pub fn column_read_values_mut(&mut self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &mut Expr> {
        self.columns.iter_mut().filter_map(|c| c.read.as_mut()).chain(self.column_reads.iter_mut().map(|(_,r)| &mut r.value))
    }

//! Bind historical arguments without requiring them in the current row type.
use super::*;
use crate::{ast, diag::{type_error, unknown}, types::{Ty, scalar_to_column}};
use snapback4_core::schema::backfill::Backfill;
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

fn bind_one(
    catalog:&Catalog,schema:&mut Schema,diagnostics:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,d:&ast::BackfillDecl,down:bool,
    names:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>,outputs:&mut BTreeSet<(String,String)>,
) {
    let word=if down{"down projection"}else{"backfill"};
    if !names.insert((d.table.clone(),d.name.clone())) { diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,format!("duplicate {word} name on this table"),"give each historical transform its own name")); return; }
    let decl = ast::ProgramDecl { delivery: None, held:false, predicts:false, server:false, kind:ast::ProgramKind::Query,
        name: format!("{}{}{}",if down{"down"}else{"backfill"},d.table,d.name), args:d.args.clone(), prelude:vec![],
        body:vec![ast::Stmt::Return { value:d.value.clone(), site:d.site.clone() }], native:false, site:d.site.clone() };
    let Some(bound) = program::Binder::bind_historical(catalog,schema,"schema",&decl,diagnostics) else {return};
    let Ty::Object(fields) = &bound.result else { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("{word} returns an object patch"),"{ column: historicalValue }")); return; };
    let Some(table) = schema.table(&d.table) else {diagnostics.push(unknown(&d.site,format!("{word} table is not declared"),"declare the current table"));return};
    for (c,ty) in fields {
        if !down {if let Some(kind) = table.columns.get(c) {
            if !ty.assignable_to(&Ty::from_column(kind)) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("backfill {}.{c} returns {}, expected {}",d.table,ty.name(),kind),"return a value fitting the candidate column")); }
        }}
        if !outputs.insert((d.table.clone(),c.clone())) { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,format!("{word}s overlap on {}.{c}",d.table),"combine simultaneous outputs in one declaration")); }
    }
    let fill = Backfill { table:d.table.clone(), name:d.name.clone(), inputs:d.args.iter().map(|a|(a.name.clone(),scalar_to_column(&a.type_))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(), outputs:fields.keys().cloned().collect(), program:bound.program };
    let admitted=if down {fill.admit_down(schema)}else{fill.admit(schema)};
    if let Err(why) = admitted { diagnostics.push(type_error(&d.site,why,"use deterministic typed fields and a scalar patch").guide("deploy")); }
    if down {schema.downfills.push(fill.normalized())}else{schema.backfills.push(fill.normalized())}
}

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &mut Schema, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.extractions) {
        let extraction=snapback4_core::schema::Extraction{child:d.child.clone(),parent:d.parent.clone(),reference:d.reference.clone(),fields:d.fields.iter().cloned().collect()};
        schema.extractions.push(extraction);
    }
    let mut forward_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut forward_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.backfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);}
    let mut down_names=BTreeSet::new();let mut down_outputs=BTreeSet::new();
    for d in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.downfills) {bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,d,true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);}
    for table in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.tables) {
        if let Some(old)=&table.renamed_from {
            if old==&table.name || catalog.tables.contains_key(old) || schema.table_renames.values().any(|v|v==old) {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&table.site,"table rename requires one distinct predecessor-only source","keep one name for each row lifetime"));
            } else {schema.table_renames.insert(table.name.clone(),old.clone());}
        }
        for column in table.columns.iter().filter(|c|c.renamed_from.is_some()) {
            let old=column.renamed_from.as_ref().unwrap();
            if old==&column.name || table.columns.iter().any(|c|&c.name==old) {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("rename source {}.{old} must be a distinct predecessor-only column",table.name),"remove the current old column and retain only the renamed declaration"));continue
            }
            fn asset(kind:&ast::TypeKind)->bool {match kind {ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}=>true,ast::TypeKind::List{item,..}=>asset(&item.kind),_=>false}}
            if (asset(&column.type_.kind)||matches!(&column.type_.kind,ast::TypeKind::Ref{..})) && column.maintained.is_none() && column.sequence.is_none() && column.written_by.is_none() {
                schema.asset_renames.entry(table.name.clone()).or_default().insert(column.name.clone(),old.clone());continue;
            }
            if matches!(&column.type_.kind, ast::TypeKind::Ref{..}|ast::TypeKind::Image{..}|ast::TypeKind::Video{..}|ast::TypeKind::File{..}|ast::TypeKind::List{..})
                || column.maintained.is_some() || column.sequence.is_some() || column.written_by.is_some()
            {
                diagnostics.push(type_error(&column.site,format!("column rename on {}.{} requires an authoritative scalar without reference, asset, collection, captured or lifetime ownership",table.name,column.name),"use a bridge migration that preserves the protected field's authority and lifetime"));continue
            }
            let declaration=|input:&str,output:&str,prefix:&str|ast::BackfillDecl{
                table:table.name.clone(),name:format!("$rename:{prefix}:{old}:{}",column.name),
                args:vec![ast::ArgDecl{alias:None,default:None,deprecated:false,name:input.into(),type_:column.type_.clone(),site:column.site.clone()}],
                value:ast::Expr::Object{fields:vec![ast::ObjectField{spread:false,name:output.into(),value:Some(ast::Expr::Name{name:input.into(),site:column.site.clone()}),site:column.site.clone()}],site:column.site.clone()},site:column.site.clone()
            };
            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(old,&column.name,"up"),false,&mut forward_names,&mut forward_outputs);
            bind_one(catalog,schema,diagnostics,&declaration(&column.name,old,"down"),true,&mut down_names,&mut down_outputs);
        }
    }
}

    11	    let start=text.split_inclusive('\n').take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(str::len).sum::<usize>();
    12	    start+text[start..].chars().take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize).map(char::len_utf8).sum::<usize>()
    13	}
    14	fn line_span(file: &str, text: &str, site: &crate::Site) -> Option<(usize, usize)> {
    15	    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, text);
    16	    let at = tokens.iter().position(|t| t.site == *site)?;
    17	    let end = tokens[at..].iter().find(|t| t.kind == Kind::Newline)?;
    18	    Some((
    19	        offset(text, site.line, 1),
    20	        offset(text, end.site.line, end.site.col),
    21	    ))
    22	}
    23	fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize, String) {
    24	    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
    25	    let mut end = text.len();
    26	    for (next, s) in text.lines().enumerate().skip(line as usize) {

    28	            end = offset(text, next as u32 + 1, 1);
    29	            break;
    30	        }
    31	    }
    32	    (start, end, text[start..end].to_owned())
    33	}

   163	                ));
   164	            }
   165	            // Constraints are current authority too: carry authored quotas,
   166	            // immutable fields and final-existence requirements verbatim.
   167	            for (_,site) in &old.immutable {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   168	            for cap in &old.caps {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,&cap.site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   169	            for (_,site) in &old.final_exists {if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,site){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   170	            let at=declaration(text,old.site.line).1;
   171	            for(fields,_)in &next.immutable {edits.push((at,at,format!("  immutable {}\n",fields.join(", "))));}
   172	            for cap in &next.caps {edits.push((at,at,format!("  cap {} by {}\n",cap.max,cap.by.join(", "))));}
   173	            for(expr,_)in &next.final_exists {edits.push((at,at,format!("  after insert <- {}\n",print::expression(&remap(expr)))));}
   174	            for prior in [&old.expire,&old.not_before].into_iter().flatten(){if let Some((a,b))=line_span(file,text,&prior.1){edits.push((a,b,String::new()));}}
   175	            if let Some((field,_))=&next.expire {edits.push((at,at,format!("  expire at .{field}\n")));}
   176	            if let Some((field,_))=&next.not_before {edits.push((at,at,format!("  not before .{field}\n")));}
   177	            // An authored current endpoint or guard may name a new index. Keep
   178	            // that index with the body; an equal-key old name is not an alias.
   179	            for index in carried_indexes {
   180	                if let Some((_,current_text,_))=docs.iter().find(|(_,_,d)|d.tables.iter().any(|t|t.name==next.name)) {
   181	                    if let Some((a,b))=line_span(&index.site.file,current_text,&index.site){
   182	                        let source=super::release_bridge_indexes::plain_declaration(&index.site.file,&current_text[a..b],index,old,next);
   183	                        edits.push((at,at,format!("{source}\n")));
   184	                        changes.push(format!("{}: retained current named index {} for authored guards and endpoints",old.name,index.name));
   185	                    }
   186	                }
   187	            }
   188	            let mut replaced = BTreeSet::new();
   189	            for rule in &old.rules {
   190	                let Some((start, end)) = line_span(file, text, &rule.site) else {
   191	                    continue;
   192	                };
   193	                if !replaced.insert((start, end)) {
   194	                    continue;
   195	                }
   196	                let rules = old
   197	                    .rules
   198	                    .iter()
   199	                    .filter(|r| r.site == rule.site)
   200	                    .map(|old_rule| {
   201	                        let effect = old_rule.effect.word();
   202	                        match next.rules.iter().find(|r| r.effect == old_rule.effect) {
   203	                            Some(current) => {
   204	                                if let Some(raw)=authored_read(current){return raw}
   205	                                let bindings = if current.bindings.is_empty() {
   206	                                    String::new()
   207	                                } else {
   208	                                    format!(" ({})", current.bindings.join(", "))
   209	                                };
   210	                                format!(
   211	                                    "  {effect}{bindings} <- {}",
   212	                                    print::expression(&remap(&current.value))
   213	                                )
   214	                            }
   215	                            None if old_rule.effect==ast::Effect::Read&&next.public.is_some()=>String::new(),
   216	                            None => format!("  {effect} <- deny"),
   217	                        }
   218	                    })
   219	                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()
   220	                    .join("\n");
   221	                if text[start..end] != rules {
   222	                    edits.push((start, end, rules));
   223	                    changes.push(format!(
   224	                        "{}: current authored {} authority retained",
   225	                        old.name,
   226	                        rule.effect.word()
   227	                    ));
   228	                }
   229	            }
   230	            for col in &old.columns {
   231	                let Some(next_col) = next
   232	                    .columns
   233	                    .iter()
   234	                    .find(|c| c.name == col.name || c.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&col.name))
   235	                else {
   236	                    continue;
   237	                };
   238	                let grouped = next
   239	                    .column_reads
   240	                    .iter()
   241	                    .find(|(fields, _)| fields.contains(&next_col.name))
   242	                    .map(|(_, r)| &r.value);
   243	                let Some(gate) = next_col.read.as_ref().or(grouped) else {
   244	                    continue;
   245	                };
   246	                if col.read.is_none()
   247	                    && !old
   248	                        .column_reads
   249	                        .iter()
   250	                        .any(|(fields, _)| fields.contains(&col.name))
   251	                {
   252	                    let at = declaration(text, old.site.line).1;
   253	                    edits.push((
   254	                        at,
   255	                        at,
   256	                        format!(
   257	                            "  read({}) <- {}\n",
   258	                            col.name,
   259	                            print::expression(&remap(gate))
   260	                        ),
   261	                    ));
   262	                    changes.push(format!(
   263	                        "{}.{}: retained current payload read guard",
   264	                        old.name, col.name
   265	                    ));
   266	                }
   267	            }
   268	        }
   269	        for old in &doc.programs {
   270	            let mut reads = BTreeSet::new();
   271	            let mut d = ast::Document::default();
   272	            d.programs.push(old.clone());
   273	            crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut d, &mut |e| match e {
   274	                ast::Expr::Read(r)
   275	                | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
   276	                | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => {
   277	                    reads.insert(r.table.clone());
   278	                }
   279	                ast::Expr::Write { table, .. } => {
   280	                    reads.insert(table.clone());
   281	                }
   282	                _ => (),
   283	            });
   284	            if reads.is_disjoint(&relaxed)
   285	                && reads.is_disjoint(&expanded)
   286	                && !forced_programs.contains(&super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name))
   287	            {
   288	                continue;
   289	            }
   290	            let Some((current_file, current_text, next)) = programs.get(&super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name)) else {
   291	                continue;
   292	            };
   293	            if old.kind != next.kind
   294	                || old.args.len() != next.args.len()
   295	                || old
   296	                    .args
   297	                    .iter()
   298	                    .zip(&next.args)
   299	                    .any(|(a, b)| a.name != b.name || !input_accepts(&a.type_, &b.type_))
   300	            {
   301	                continue;
   302	            }
   303	            let (_, _, mut source) = declaration(current_text, next.site.line);
   304	            let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(current_file, &source);
   305	            let mut replacements = Vec::new();
   306	            if next.name!=old.name {if let Some(name)=tokens.iter().find(|t|t.value==next.name){let at=offset(&source,name.site.line,name.site.col);replacements.push((at,at+name.value.len(),old.name.clone()));}}
   307	            let parsed = parser::parse_document(current_file, &source).0;
   308	            let mut parsed = parsed;
   309	            if let Some(endpoint)=parsed.programs.first() {
   310	                for prior in &old.args {
   311	                    let Some(alias)=&prior.alias else{continue};
   312	                    let Some(arg)=endpoint.args.iter().find(|arg|arg.name==prior.name&&arg.alias.is_none())else{continue};
   313	                    if let Ok((_,end))=super::release_bridge_current::arg_type(&source,&tokens,arg){replacements.push((end,end,format!(" alias {alias}")));}
   314	                }
   315	            }
   316	            crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut parsed, &mut |e| {
   317	                let (table, site) = match e {
   318	                    ast::Expr::Read(r)
   319	                    | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
   320	                    | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => (&r.table, &r.site),
   321	                    ast::Expr::Write { table, site, .. } => (&*table, &*site),
   322	                    _ => return,
   323	                };
   324	                if let Some(old) = aliases.get(table) {
   325	                    if let Some(token) = tokens.iter().find(|t| {
   326	                        t.site.line == site.line && t.site.col >= site.col && t.value == *table
   327	                    }) {
   328	                        let at = offset(&source, token.site.line, token.site.col);
   329	                        replacements.push((at, at + table.len(), old.clone()));
   330	                    }
   331	                }
   332	            });
   333	            replacements.sort();
   334	            replacements.dedup();
   335	            replacements.sort_by_key(|r| std::cmp::Reverse(r.0));
   336	            for (a, b, value) in replacements {
   337	                source.replace_range(a..b, &value);
   338	            }
   339	            let (a, b, _) = declaration(text, old.site.line);
   340	            edits.push((a, b, source));
   341	            changes.push(format!("operation {}: current authored compatibility endpoint preserves its argument contract and guards",super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&old.name)));
   342	        }
   343	        for fill in &doc.backfills {
   344	            let Some(current) = tables.get(&fill.table) else {
   345	                continue;
   346	            };
   347	            if let ast::Expr::Object { fields, .. } = &fill.value {
   348	                if fields
   349	                    .iter()
   350	                    .all(|f| current.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == f.name))
   351	                {
   352	                    let (a, b, _) = declaration(text, fill.site.line);
   353	                    edits.push((a, b, String::new()));
   354	                    changes.push(format!(
   355	                        "{}: retained current forward migration custody instead of historical {}",
   356	                        fill.table, fill.name
   357	                    ));
   358	                }
   359	            }
   360	        }
   361	        for down in &doc.downfills {
   362	            if docs.iter().any(|(_, _, d)| {
   363	                d.downfills
   364	                    .iter()
   365	                    .any(|f| f.table == down.table && f.name == down.name)
   366	            }) {
   367	                continue;
   368	            }
   369	            let Some(current) = tables.get(&down.table) else {
   370	                continue;
   371	            };
   372	            if let ast::Expr::Object { fields, .. } = &down.value {
   373	                if fields
   374	                    .iter()
   375	                    .all(|f| current.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == f.name))
   376	                {
   377	                    let (a, b, _) = declaration(text, down.site.line);
   378	                    edits.push((a, b, String::new()));
   379	                    changes.push(format!("{}: obsolete down {} stays historical; visible view keeps current native values",down.table,down.name));
   380	                }
   381	            }
   382	        }
   383	        for (current_file, current_text, current_doc) in &docs {
   384	            for backfill in &current_doc.backfills {
   385	                if !doc.tables.iter().any(|t| t.name == backfill.table) {
   386	                    continue;
   387	                }
   388	                let (_, _, source) = declaration(current_text, backfill.site.line);
   389	                edits.push((text.len(), text.len(), format!("\n{source}")));
   390	                changes.push(format!(
   391	                    "{}: retained current migration {}",
   392	                    backfill.table, backfill.name
   393	                ));
   394	                let _ = current_file;
   395	            }
   396	        }
   397	        edits.sort_by_key(|e| std::cmp::Reverse((e.0, e.1)));
   398	        for (a, b, value) in edits {
   399	            text.replace_range(a..b, &value);
   400	        }
   401	    }
   402	    relaxed.extend(expanded);
   403	    relaxed
   404	}
   405	
   406	fn current_default(file:&str,text:&str,old:&ast::ColumnDecl,new:&ast::ColumnDecl)->Option<(usize,usize,String)> {
   407	    let authored=|column:&ast::ColumnDecl|column.default.as_ref().map(print::expression);
   408	    if authored(old)==authored(new){return None;}
   409	    let replacement=authored(new).map(|value|format!("default {value}")).unwrap_or_default();
   410	    if let Some(value)=&old.default {
   411	        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);
   412	        let at=tokens.iter().position(|token|token.site==*value.site())?;
   413	        let keyword=at.checked_sub(1).filter(|i|tokens[*i].value=="default")?;
   414	        let end=at+if matches!(value,ast::Expr::Unary{..}){2}else{1};
   415	        let end=offset(text,tokens.get(end)?.site.line,tokens.get(end)?.site.col).min(text.len());
   416	        Some((offset(text,tokens[keyword].site.line,tokens[keyword].site.col),text[..end].trim_end().len(),replacement))
   417	    }else{
   418	        let(_,end)=super::release_bridge::type_span(file,text,old)?;
   419	        Some((end,end,format!(" {replacement}")))
   420	    }
   421	}
   422	
   423	pub(super) fn input_accepts(old: &ast::ScalarType, new: &ast::ScalarType) -> bool {
   424	    if old == new {
   425	        return true;
   426	    }
   427	    if old.optional && !new.optional {
   428	        return false;
   429	    }
   430	    use ast::TypeKind::*;
   431	    let bounds = |k: &ast::TypeKind| match k {
   432	        Int => Some((i64::MIN, i64::MAX)),
   433	        IntRange { min, max } => Some((min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN), max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX))),
   434	        _ => None,
   435	    };
   436	    match (bounds(&old.kind), bounds(&new.kind)) {
   437	        (Some((a, b)), Some((x, y))) => x <= a && y >= b,
   438	        _ => match (&old.kind,&new.kind){(Enum{variants:a},Enum{variants:b})=>a.iter().all(|v|b.contains(v)),_=>old.kind==new.kind},
   439	    }
   440	}

   448	        let current = "table items:\n  n: int 0..10\n  category: enum('free','paid')\n  insert <- allow\n  update (old,next) <- old.category = next.category\n  online only\nmutation add(n: int 0..10):\n  return insert items {n,category:'free' if n = 0 else 'paid'}\nbackfill items classify(n: int 0..10): {category:'free' if n = 0 else 'paid'}\n";

   485	    }

fn declaration(text:&str,line:u32)->String {
    let lines:Vec<_>=text.lines().collect();let start=line.saturating_sub(1) as usize;let mut end=start+1;
    while end<lines.len() {let s=lines[end];if !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--"){break;}end+=1;}
    lines[start..end].join("\n")+"\n"
}
// Rewrite only structural table references; strings, column names and bound
// rule parameters retain their spelling and policy meaning.
pub(super) fn retained_declaration(file:&str,text:&str,table:&ast::TableDecl,aliases:&BTreeMap<String,String>)->Result<String,String> {
    let mut source=declaration(text,table.site.line);
    let mut decl=parser::parse_document(file,&source).0.tables.into_iter().next().ok_or("missing retained table declaration")?;
    let mut edits=Vec::new();
    fn remap_type(t:&mut ast::ScalarType,aliases:&BTreeMap<String,String>) {
        match &mut t.kind {TypeKind::Ref{table}=>{if let Some(name)=aliases.get(table){*table=name.clone();}},TypeKind::List{item,..}=>remap_type(item,aliases),_=>()}
    }
    for column in &decl.columns {
        let mut type_=column.type_.clone();remap_type(&mut type_,aliases);
        if type_!=column.type_ {if let Some((start,end))=type_span(file,&source,column){edits.push((start,end,print::scalar_type(&type_)));}}
    }
    let mut visit=|expr:&mut ast::Expr| {
        if let ast::Expr::Read(read)|ast::Expr::Exists{read,..}|ast::Expr::Created{read,..}=expr {
            if let Some(name)=aliases.get(&read.table) {let start=offset(&source,read.site.line,read.site.col);edits.push((start,start+read.table.len(),name.clone()));}
        }
    };
    for rule in decl.rules.iter_mut().chain(decl.column_reads.iter_mut().map(|(_,r)|r)) {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut rule.value,&mut visit);}
    for column in &mut decl.columns {
        for expr in [&mut column.read,&mut column.default].into_iter().flatten(){crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
        if let Some(expr)=column.maintained.as_mut().and_then(|a|a.filter.as_mut()){crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
    }
    for (expr,_) in &mut decl.final_exists {crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(expr,&mut visit);}
    // Sync targets are structural table probes outside the expression AST.
    if let Some(sync)=&decl.sync {
        let (tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,&source);
        // Lexer continuation rules keep indented `or` arms in this logical
        // declaration even when their physical line differs from `sync`.
        if let Some(start)=tokens.iter().position(|t|t.site==sync.site) {
            let end=tokens[start..].iter().position(|t|matches!(t.kind,Kind::Newline|Kind::Eof)).map_or(tokens.len(),|n|start+n);
            for pair in tokens[start..end].windows(2) {if pair[0].kind==Kind::Word && pair[1].value=="[" {if let Some(name)=aliases.get(&pair[0].value){let start=offset(&source,pair[0].site.line,pair[0].site.col);edits.push((start,start+pair[0].value.len(),name.clone()));}}}
        }
    }
    edits.sort();edits.dedup();
    if edits.iter().any(|(a,b,_)|a>b||*b>source.len()||!source.is_char_boundary(*a)||!source.is_char_boundary(*b))
        || edits.windows(2).any(|pair|pair[0].1>pair[1].0) {
        return Err("unsupported or overlapping retained-table source spans".into());
    }
    edits.sort_by_key(|e|std::cmp::Reverse(e.0));
    for (start,end,value) in edits {source.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
    Ok(source)
}
fn join(old:&ast::ScalarType,new:&ast::ScalarType,tables:&BTreeMap<String,(&String,&String,ast::TableDecl)>)->Option<ast::ScalarType> {
    let kind=match (&old.kind,&new.kind) {
        (TypeKind::Int|TypeKind::IntRange{..},TypeKind::Int|TypeKind::IntRange{..})=>{
            let bounds=|k:&TypeKind|match k{TypeKind::IntRange{min,max}=>(min.unwrap_or(i64::MIN),max.unwrap_or(i64::MAX)),_=>(i64::MIN,i64::MAX)};
            let(a,b)=bounds(&old.kind);let(x,y)=bounds(&new.kind);let(min,max)=(a.min(x),b.max(y));
            if min==i64::MIN && max==i64::MAX {TypeKind::Int}else{TypeKind::IntRange{min:(min!=i64::MIN).then_some(min),max:(max!=i64::MAX).then_some(max)}}
### 20.2 Writer-enforced visibility

Default rollback, even without a registered view, admits zero unacknowledged
hidden predecessor-table rows. Candidate preview also refuses uncovered hiding
on reverse snapshots. Future bad projections refuse selection under the writer.
Preview populations are privileged retained-row counts, not viewer guarantees.

The initial intentional-hiding spelling is:
`rollback N hides TABLE where COLUMN = null because 'REASON'`.
The candidate column must be nullable, the predecessor column nonnullable, and
table/column spellings identical. N is the immutable original. No arbitrary
predicate, blanket exemption or rename combination is admitted. Its view keeps
the null restriction while joining the nonnull domain: nullable 0..1000 versus
required 0..100 becomes required 0..1000, preserving widened nonnull values.
The nonempty reason travels in immutable metadata and repeats on selection.

Under the actual writer transaction, capture the before image, run full adoption,
then compare every retained hidden predecessor row. Only acknowledged null rows
and the actual hidden-reference closure rooted in them are allowed; unrelated
hidden rows refuse atomically. The closure graph contains only scalar/optional
nominal Ref cells in before-image active rows, as typed by the source schema;
ids are aligned through the catalog identity. An edge qualifies only when its
parent was also active before, is actually retained-hidden after adoption, and
has no invalid reference-lifetime marker. No list, synthetic extraction anchor,
asset, dormant overlay, cascade or set-null declaration invents an edge. A
renamed identity uses its same catalog number. Before-hidden rows are outside
this newly-hidden population; newer-only tables are distinguished. The snapshot
preview is advisory; concurrent writes cannot bypass activation revalidation.
Acknowledgment never changes physical preservation or permits broader read/write
authority. After collecting the actual newly hidden table set, the writer rejects
negative existence authority, reference comparisons that can admit absence, or
population-tainted requirements/control flow. Positive existence requirements
may only become denials. A direct point assertion may discharge absence taint:
if its row is hidden it aborts before subsequent work; its surviving field values
remain unchanged. Writes to affected existing targets require that same exact
point-presence guard. Transitive bindings and compiler-expanded helper bodies
are followed; unknown paths refuse. Tainted branch/loop/conditional-write keys AND persisted write values,
negative/count-based guard dependencies, nested job-query scope and maintained
populations depending on hidden sources are conservatively outside this initial
acknowledgment form. This is a closed syntactic admission check, not an inferred
semantic implication or a finite-snapshot security proof. Explicit reasons do
not bypass it. Source may retain the safety witness in a visible bridge instead.
Ordinary hidden-row occupancy/retention laws remain explicit in the preview;
intentional hiding supplies no promise of automatic forward conflict resolution.

Point-presence proofs discharge taint only for subsequent work in program
order on that exact table/key. A guard cannot justify any earlier tainted read
or write. Mutations/jobs retain their existing atomic transaction law: a later
refusal rolls back all prior writes in the operation, including work before a
point assertion. The population checker does not weaken that transaction law.

### 20.3 Edited transformation outputs

An edited in-place migration output reverses through an applicable down on its
assembled current image, or stays exact if the compiled selected view's full
type/constraints accept that physical value. This never widens a schema per row,
coerces, truncates, null-fills or invents defaults. Otherwise selection refuses.
Original preimage, actual authored newer output, prior computation and physical
old-view presence are separate evidence; edited output never gains native birth.

Forward validates old-view field presence/null/default/lifetime evidence first.
Preserving authored output requires unchanged explicit arguments AND freshly
unchanged computation, including point/existence lookups. Changed inputs require
exact deterministic agreement with authored output or a real conflict printing
competing values. Unedited outputs retain preimage precedence; receipts state
when older views read stale until forward. Repeated and partially retired cycles,
invalid lifetimes, pruned evidence and meter cuts obey the same atomic laws.

### 20.4 Reference renames and asset extraction

Reference renamed-from transports the existing physical cell and reference
lifetime bindings, including retained/narrowed images. It never recaptures a
deleted/reused parent or captured/engine-owned field. Both aliases at once and
unequal destination cells refuse. After identity alignment, nominal target,
nullability, bounds, on-delete and capture shape must be exactly equal; widening
is a separate migration, not part of this rename.

`extract CHILD from PARENT via REFERENCE: {newField: oldField}` is a declarative
migration, not an operation or cross-table rename. It creates one receipt-owned
initial child per source row; child's deterministic id equals parent id in the
child namespace. The child has a required reference to parent with cascade or
restrict deletion; keep/set-null are outside the initial form. Independent active
or dormant destination history refuses. At least one mapped asset is required,
with identical full source/child type. Parent must remove that source asset
column. Other copied fields must be explicitly immutable scalar source fields;
mutable, captured, maintained and nominal-reference copies refuse. Copies must
continue to agree both directions. Other required child columns need authored
defaults. Receipt identity uses numeric parent/child catalog identities and declared column
origins, so table, asset-column and uncaptured reference-column aliases do not
create a new migration or recapture an anchor. Typed schema normalization updates
extraction table/reference slots and preserves source/destination column strings.
Destination admission inspects active rows, dormant rows/cells and competing
receipts. Reentry requires its existing receipt-owned child and valid anchor;
pruned/deleted child history is never recreated from metadata.

Future independent children with distinct ids are allowed; extraction is not
unique-parent. Existing durable id reservation and duplicate-key admission apply
to receipt-owned/dormant children exactly as to ordinary rows. The normal mint
never overwrites an occupied id; an explicitly forced collision refuses. Neither
rollback nor deleting receipt metadata frees a still-retained row id.

Asset ledger home and physical asset cell move together, without reuploading or
changing bytes. The former active cell and equal dormant overlay are removed;
a distinct overlay refuses rather than being discarded. Private displaced-cell
receipt evidence persists separately from active cells. Reverse removes the
child asset cell while retaining identity/scalars and newer-only children. Later
forward uses the current old-view asset, including replacement/optional clearing,
never reviving released receipt bytes or creating a second ledger home.

A native child without a receipt reverses only when exactly one total candidate child
exists for that parent, and that child has a valid private parent-lifetime anchor. Anchors are captured while
extraction is active, including absent-parent invalid anchors; unchanged writes
never recapture invalid evidence. Older independent relations cannot qualify
retroactively. Deletion/id reuse, reparenting and ambiguity refuse; no arbitrary
photo selection. Receipt-owned children likewise require valid lifetime evidence.

For row-dependent audiences the child must read through its parent with identical
parent policy; otherwise source/child read policies must be identical and row-
independent, or destination denies reads. Existing payload gates must match.
Reverse must preserve the current effective child/parent/column audience or
refuse with guarded-bridge advice. These are closed admission rules, not a
policy implication prover. Native/ref compatibility does not confer byte access.

Actual activation revalidates active AND dormant destination emptiness, regular
byte presence/ledger length, full types, scalar copies, parent binding, anchor
validity and reverse ambiguity inside adoption under command locking and CAS.
Preview runs this transaction on a private copy; its counts cannot authorize a
later activation. Failure rolls back evidence, format, homes, selection and
generation together. Devices execute no migration metadata or home authority:
authenticated serving resolves the current server ledger home and current row/
column policy. Existing device generation and media-retention receipts remain;
this introduces no retroactive cache-erasure claim.

Store format 9 fences edited-output/reference/extraction AND compiled-view
registry custody from older runtimes. Every successful release adoption upgrades
to 9 transactionally, before prepared registry entries become selectable. Older
CLIs must not mistake view ids for ordinary latest or skip visibility policy.
Every hot activation request from this CLI carries the explicit registry-custody
feature field, including exact/original-source selection, an empty registry and
an existing format-8 store. The field covers all format-9 adoption laws, including
edited outputs; it is not conditional on a visible view. Updated hosts reject
activation clients omitting it. Older hosts reject that unknown field before
admission; restart serve with the updated runtime. Updated hosts always require
it before activation admission, with no conditional old-wire-shape exception.
This prevents an older engine from executing any adoption without the new laws.
Unchanged no-op deployments without new custody can stay at 8. Format 8 opens;
failed adoption leaves its old format unchanged; 9 survives
old-source selection. Capabilities release-child-extraction-v1 and
release-reference-renames-v1 fence schema admission. Device projections omit
migration metadata while retaining ordinary current tables and through policies.

### 20.5 Presentation and qualification

Human deploy output starts with verdict/security FIX/test status, followed by
adoption/rollback facts. Result advice groups a changed source column with its
affected operations once. Security review is last, scoped to changed declarations,
ranked and capped at five advisory notes, with omitted count. Reachable FIX
witnesses are never capped/suppressed. A trailing verdict supports tail readers.
JSON carries every compatibility detail, report, test status and view mapping.
Campaign matching/stale/missing/incomplete state and age remain explicit. Finite
tests never certify universal security. SEC7 owns ordinary check ranking/gates.

Readiness requires blind Muse and fresh Codex READY reviews of the same revision,
retained verbatim with provenance. Qualification covers cycles/conflicts, optional
money, image/file homes, deleted/reused references, view failure/CAS/restart,
bakeoff copies and packed guide cases. This record claims no unrun test or
publication and authorizes no push/version bump.

//! Seeks and row-local scan predicates.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.000.001#4 — retain the binder's actual choice for why, never infer a new lookup or rewrite.
    pub(super) fn record_selection(&mut self, site: &str, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, result: &str, reason: &str) {
        let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
        let bindings = index.columns.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(position, column)| {
            let key = if named { read.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column)) } else { read.keys.get(position) }?;
            Some(format!("{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value)))
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        let origin = if self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())) { "synthesized; " } else { "" };
        let source = if origin.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("; read: {}", self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(read))) };
        let mut selection = format!("selected {} ({bindings}); result: {result}; {origin}{reason}{source}", index.name);
        if !self.variants.is_empty() {
            let when = self.variants.iter().map(|(name, present)| format!("{} {}", name.trim_start_matches("$intentPresent:").trim_start_matches("$intentSearch:"), if *present { "present" } else { "absent" })).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
            selection = format!("when {when}: {selection}");
        }
        self.selections.entry(site.into()).and_modify(|prior| { if !prior.contains(&selection) { prior.push_str("; "); prior.push_str(&selection); } }).or_insert(selection);
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — a covered total unique key reads one row; additional named equalities check that row.
    pub(super) fn read_value(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Expr> {
        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
        for key in &read.keys { if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::super::row_views::predicate_field(info, field, &read.site)?; } }
        if !read.seeks.is_empty() { return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek needs a bounded page", "t[prefix, col >= start] first 50 by index")); }
        let mut point = read.clone();
        if point.keys.is_empty() && info.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) {
            point.keys.push(ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), site: read.site.clone() }, site: read.site.clone() });
            point.index = Some("byGroup".into());
        } else if point.keys.is_empty() {
            return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{}[] reads nothing; a point read needs a key", read.table), format!("{}[id]", read.table)));
        }
        let implicit_group = mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && point.index.is_none();
        if implicit_group { point.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
        let (mut keys, index, mut selection) = self.select_index(info, &point, mode, out)?;
        super::super::point_key::check(info, &index, read, &keys.iter().map(|(column, _, ty)|
            (column.as_deref(), !ty.is_optional() && *ty != Ty::Unknown)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), false)?;
        if implicit_group { selection = "complete maintained group key".into(); }
        let extra = keys.len() > index.columns.len();
        let mut equalities = Vec::new();
        if extra {
            for (column, value, ty) in &mut keys {
                let column = column.as_ref().expect("named key");
                if column != "id" && !info.columns.contains_key(column) {
                    return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
                }
                let expected = self.key_ty(info, &index, column);
                if !ty.comparable(&expected) { return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
                *value = self.snapshot(value.clone(), out);
                if !index.columns.contains(column) { equalities.push((column.clone(), value.clone())); }
            }
            keys.retain(|(column, _, _)| index.columns.contains(column.as_ref().unwrap()));
        }
        let key = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
        let location = self.site();
        self.record_selection(&location, read, &index, if mode == ReadMode::Exists { "exists" } else if info.maintained.is_some() { "scalar" } else { "row or null" }, &selection);
        self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (read.site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
        let mut point = Read { site: location, table: read.table.clone(), index: index.name, key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
        if mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_none() {
            self.optional_lookup_note(read, &point);
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.4 — only a caller-proved existence read may use the whole owner partition.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && equalities.is_empty() {
            super::super::own_keys::existence(self.schema, &mut point);
        }
        if equalities.is_empty() { return Ok(if mode == ReadMode::Exists { Expr::Exists(point) } else { Expr::Get(point) }); }
        let row = self.temp("probe");
        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Get(point) });
        let mut condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied aggregate carries a null value, not a present group.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && info.maintained.is_some() {
            let present = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value")), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(present) };
        }
        for (column, value) in equalities {
            let test = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), &column)), right: Box::new(value) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(test) };
        }
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists { return Ok(condition); }
        out.push(Stmt::If { condition, then_body: Vec::new(), else_body: vec![Stmt::Assign { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) }] });
        Ok(ir::var(&row))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.3 — a seek constrains the first index column after the pinned prefix.
    pub(super) fn seek(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<ir::Range>> {
        if read.seeks.is_empty() { return Ok(None); }
        let mut range = ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None };
        for bound in &read.seeks {
            if index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) != Some(&bound.column) || read.matching.is_some() {
                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek compares the first unpinned column of a plain index", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
            }
            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column);
            let (value, ty) = self.field_value(&ObjectField { spread: false, name: bound.column.clone(), value: Some(bound.value.clone()), site: bound.site.clone() }, &expected, out)?;
            if !ty.comparable(&expected) || ty.is_optional() {
                let endpoint = print::expression(&bound.value);
                let rewrite = if ty.is_optional() {
                    format!("put the read inside `if {endpoint} != null:`, then `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
                } else {
                    format!("use a present {} endpoint in `{} where .{} {} {endpoint}`", expected.required().name(), read.table, bound.column, bound.op)
                };
                return Err(type_error(&bound.site, format!("seek on {}.{} expects present {}, got {}", read.table, bound.column, expected.required().name(), ty.name()), rewrite));
            }
            let slot = match bound.op.as_str() { ">" => &mut range.gt, ">=" => &mut range.gte, "<" => &mut range.lt, "<=" => &mut range.lte, _ => unreachable!("parser seek comparison") };
            if slot.is_some() { return Err(type_error(&bound.site, "a seek bound is repeated", "write one lower and one upper bound")); }
            *slot = Some(Box::new(self.snapshot(value, out)));
        }
        if (range.gt.is_some() && range.gte.is_some()) || (range.lt.is_some() && range.lte.is_some()) {
            return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek has at most one lower and one upper bound", "t[prefix, col >= start, col < end] first 50 by index"));
        }
        // Null sorts below every present value. An upper-only nullable seek
        // must skip the entire null segment, including its id suffixes.
        if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none()
            && read.seeks.first().is_some_and(|bound| self.key_ty(info, index, &bound.column).is_optional()) {
            range.gt = Some(Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)));

     1	//! Independent policy follows the complete authored current execution closure.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#20-visible-rollback-views-roll — no restored workflow may bypass current independent intent.

    14	pub(super) fn prepare(target: &[Source], current: &[Source], guarded_fallback: &mut bool) -> Bridge {
    15	    *guarded_fallback=false;
    16	    let old = crate::compile(target);
    17	    let next = crate::compile(current);
    18	    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    19	        return Bridge {
    20	            sources: target.to_vec(),
    21	            changes: vec![],
    22	            diagnostics: old.diagnostics,
    23	        };
    24	    }
    25	    if !next.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    26	        return Bridge {
    27	            sources: current.to_vec(),
    28	            changes: vec![],
    29	            diagnostics: next.diagnostics,
    30	        };
    31	    }
    32	    if contracts(&old, &next) {
    33	        return retained(current.to_vec(), false);
    34	    }
    35	    match alpha(target, current) {
    36	        Ok(Some(sources)) => {
    37	            let bound = crate::compile(&sources);
    38	            if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    39	                return Bridge {
    40	                    sources,
    41	                    changes: vec![],
    42	                    diagnostics: bound.diagnostics,
    43	                };
    44	            }
    45	            if contracts(&old, &bound) {
    46	                return retained(sources, true);
    47	            }
    48	        }
    49	        Err(error) => {*guarded_fallback=error.unsupported;return refused(current, &error.reason)},
    50	        Ok(None) => {}
    51	    }
    52	    *guarded_fallback=true;
    53	    refused(current,"the complete current workflow does not retain every predecessor table, field, operation and accepted input")
    54	}
    55	fn retained(sources: Vec<Source>, alpha: bool) -> Bridge {
    56	    Bridge {sources,diagnostics:vec![],changes:vec![format!("current independent intent: retained the complete current authored schema, defaults, programs, helpers, jobs and policy declarations{}; predecessor names and accepted inputs remain available; review current results in the old UI",if alpha {" under declared table identity renames"}else{""})]}
    57	}
    58	fn refused(current: &[Source], reason: &str) -> Bridge {
    59	    Bridge {sources:current.to_vec(),changes:vec![],diagnostics:vec![crate::diag::type_error(&crate::Site::default(),format!("rollback view cannot preserve current independent intent: {reason}"),"author a compatible guarded source retaining the predecessor fields and inputs, truthful migrations and the current policy; an acknowledgment does not authorize restoring an unsafe workflow").guide("deploy")]}
    60	}
