    62	/// A loosened current workflow may omit a predecessor's nullable, default-free
    63	/// field rather than invent its value. All current schema and executable
    64	/// declarations stay intact; old wire aliases are the only source additions.
    65	pub(super) fn guarded(target:&[Source],current:&[Source])->Bridge {
    66	    let normalized=match alpha(target,current) {
    67	        Ok(sources)=>sources,
    68	        Err(error)=>return refused(current,&error.reason),
    69	    };
    70	    let current=normalized.as_deref().unwrap_or(current);
    71	    let old=crate::compile(target);let next=crate::compile(current);
    72	    if !old.diagnostics.is_empty()||!next.diagnostics.is_empty(){return refused(current,"both authored sources must compile before preparing a guarded view");}
    73	    if !table_contracts(&old,&next,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow needs every predecessor table and required/defaulted field; author a truthful reverse projection or compatible guarded source");}
    74	    let mut sources=current.to_vec();let mut changes=vec!["guarded loosened view: retained the complete current schema, defaults, helpers, programs, jobs, aliases and independent intent; omitted nullable default-free predecessor fields are absent results, never invented values; review current results in the old UI".into()];
    75	    if normalized.is_some(){changes.push("declared table identities: complete current guarded workflow retained under predecessor spellings, without rewriting business strings or weakening authority".into());}
    76	    for(file,text)in &mut sources {
    77	        let doc=parser::parse_document(file,text).0;
    78	        let Some((prior_file,prior_text))=target.iter().find(|(f,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(f,"")==super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,""))else{continue};
    79	        let prior=parser::parse_document(prior_file,prior_text).0;
    80	        let(tokens,_)=lexer::lex(file,text);let mut edits=Vec::new();let mut extra=String::new();let mut restored=BTreeMap::new();
    81	        // Retain a missing old exported alias only to an authored current
    82	        // endpoint (including an explicitly authored current alias target).
    83	        // Existing/rebound current aliases remain exactly current-authored.
    84	        for(name,target,_)in &prior.aliases {
    85	            let qualified=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name);
    86	            if next.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==qualified){continue;}
    87	            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target).or_else(||doc.aliases.iter().find(|(alias,_,_)|alias==target).and_then(|(_,target,_)|doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)));
    88	            if let Some(endpoint)=endpoint{extra.push_str(&format!("alias {name} = {}\n",endpoint.name));restored.insert(qualified,endpoint.name.clone());}
    89	        }
    90	        for previous in &old.programs {
    91	            let qualified=&previous.program.name;
    92	            let endpoint=doc.programs.iter().find(|p|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&p.name)==*qualified).or_else(|| {
    93	                let target=doc.aliases.iter().find(|(name,_,_)|super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,name)==*qualified).map(|(_,target,_)|target).or_else(||restored.get(qualified))?;
    94	                doc.programs.iter().find(|p|p.name==*target)
    95	            });
    96	            let Some(endpoint)=endpoint else{continue};
    97	            let actual=super::release_bridge::operation_name(file,&endpoint.name);
    98	            let Some(current)=next.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==*qualified||p.program.name==actual)else{continue};
    99	            for(alias,owner)in &previous.program.arg_aliases {
   100	                if current.program.argument(alias).is_some(){continue;}
   101	                let Some((canonical,_))=current.program.argument(owner)else{continue};
   102	                let Some(arg)=endpoint.args.iter().find(|arg|arg.name==canonical&&arg.alias.is_none())else{continue};
   103	                if let Ok((_,end))=arg_type(text,&tokens,arg){edits.push((end,end,format!(" alias {alias}")));changes.push(format!("operation {qualified}: retained wire alias {alias} on {canonical}"));}
   104	            }
   105	        }
   106	        edits.sort();edits.dedup();edits.sort_by_key(|edit|std::cmp::Reverse(edit.0));
   107	        for(start,end,value)in edits{text.replace_range(start..end,&value);}
   108	        if !extra.is_empty(){if !text.ends_with('\n'){text.push('\n');}text.push_str(&extra);}
   109	    }
   110	    let bound=crate::compile(&sources);
   111	    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty(){return Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:bound.diagnostics};}
   112	    if !table_contracts(&old,&bound,true)||!endpoint_contracts(&old,&bound,true){return refused(current,"the guarded current workflow cannot retain a predecessor query or a retained export's wire aliases and distinct inputs; author a compatible guarded endpoint with the old accepted arguments");}
   113	    for(name,table)in &old.schema.tables {
   114	        for column in table.columns.keys().filter(|column|!bound.schema.tables[name].columns.contains_key(*column)) {
   115	            if let Some(current)=renamed_column(&old,&bound,name,column){changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: declared identity is now {name}.{current}; guarded view uses the current spelling and result semantics; review the old UI"));}
   116	            else{changes.push(format!("{name}.{column}: removed nullable default-free field remains absent in this guarded view; historical cells stay retained; review the old UI result"));}
   117	        }
   118	    }
   119	    for previous in &old.programs {
   120	        if !bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.name==previous.program.name){changes.push(format!("operation {}: paused because no compatible current authored write endpoint exists; old calls return E_OP; author a guarded compatibility endpoint if this old workflow is still needed",previous.program.name));}
   121	    }
   122	    Bridge{sources,changes,diagnostics:vec![]}
   123	}
   124	
   125	pub(super) fn contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound)->bool {
   126	    table_contracts(old,new,false)&&endpoint_contracts(old,new,false)
   127	}
   128	fn table_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,nullable_gaps:bool)->bool {
   129	    old.schema.tables.iter().all(|(name,table)|new.schema.tables.get(name).is_some_and(|next| {
   130	        table.columns.iter().all(|(column,kind)|next.columns.contains_key(column)
   131	            || (nullable_gaps&&renamed_column(old,new,name,column).is_some())
   132	            || (nullable_gaps&&kind.is_optional()&&!table.defaults.contains_key(column)&&!table.engine_field(column)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(column)))
   133	    }))
   134	}
   135	fn endpoint_contracts(old:&crate::Bound,new:&crate::Bound,pause_removed_writes:bool)->bool {
   136	    old.programs.iter().all(|p|new.programs.iter().find(|n|n.program.name==p.program.name)
   137	        .map_or_else(||pause_removed_writes&&matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action),|n|endpoint_contract(&p.program,&n.program)))
   138	}
   139	pub(super) fn endpoint_contract(old:&snapback4_core::Program,new:&snapback4_core::Program)->bool {
   140	    if old.kind!=new.kind{return false;}
   141	    let mut positions=BTreeSet::new();
   142	    for(name,kind)in &old.args {
   143	        let Some((target,next))=new.argument(name)else{return false};
   144	        if !accepts(kind,next)||!positions.insert(target){return false;}
   145	        if !old.arg_aliases.iter().filter(|(_,owner)|*owner==name).all(|(alias,_)|new.argument(alias).is_some_and(|(canonical,next)|canonical==target&&accepts(kind,next))){return false;}
   146	    }
   147	    new.args.iter().all(|(name,kind)|positions.contains(name.as_str())||matches!(kind,ArgType::Optional(_)))
   148	}
   149	fn renamed_column<'a>(old:&crate::Bound,new:&'a crate::Bound,table:&str,column:&str)->Option<&'a str> {
   150	    let next=new.schema.table(table)?;
   151	    if let Some((name,_))=new.schema.asset_renames.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|(name,prior)|prior.as_str()==column&&next.columns.contains_key(*name)){return Some(name.as_str());}
   152	    if let Some(prior)=old.schema.asset_renames.get(table).and_then(|m|m.get(column)) {
   153	        if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
   154	    }
   155	    for transform in &new.schema.backfills {
   156	        if transform.table==table&&transform.inputs.len()==1&&transform.inputs.contains_key(column)&&transform.outputs.len()==1 {
   157	            let name=transform.outputs.iter().next()?;
   158	            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{column}:{name}")&&next.columns.contains_key(name){return Some(name.as_str());}
   159	        }
   160	    }
   161	    for transform in &old.schema.backfills {
   162	        if transform.table==table&&transform.outputs.len()==1&&transform.outputs.contains(column)&&transform.inputs.len()==1 {
   163	            let prior=transform.inputs.keys().next()?;
   164	            if transform.name==format!("$rename:up:{prior}:{column}") {
   165	                if let Some((name,_))=next.columns.get_key_value(prior){return Some(name.as_str());}
   166	            }
   167	        }
   168	    }
   169	    None
   170	}
   171	// This is inclusion of wire domains for an unchanged authored current body,
   172	// not synthesis of a conversion or permission to reinterpret old arguments.
   173	fn accepts(old: &ArgType, new: &ArgType) -> bool {
   174	    if old == new {
   175	        return true;
   176	    }
   177	    match (old, new) {
   178	        (ArgType::Optional(a), ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(a, b),
   179	        (_, ArgType::Optional(b)) => accepts(old, b),
   180	        (ArgType::Optional(_), _) => false,
   181	        (
   182	            ArgType::Text {
   183	                min: a,
   184	                max: b,
   185	                format: f,
   186	            },
   187	            ArgType::Text {
   188	                min: x,
   189	                max: y,
   190	                format: g,
   191	            },
   192	        ) => f == g && x <= a && b <= y,
   193	        (ArgType::Enum { variants: a }, ArgType::Enum { variants: b }) => {
   194	            a.iter().all(|v| b.contains(v))
   195	        }
   196	        (
   197	            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
   198	            ArgType::List { .. } | ArgType::ListRange { .. },
   199	        ) => {
   200	            let parts = |kind: &ArgType| match kind {
   201	                ArgType::List { of, max } => (0, *max, of.clone()),
   202	                ArgType::ListRange { of, min, max } => (*min, *max, of.clone()),
   203	                _ => unreachable!(),
   204	            };
   205	            let (a, b, element) = parts(old);
   206	            let (x, y, next) = parts(new);
   207	            x <= a
   208	                && b <= y
   209	                && match (element, next) {
   210	                    (None, None) => true,
   211	                    (Some(a), Some(b)) => accepts(&a, &b),
   212	                    _ => false,
   213	                }
   214	        }
   215	        (ArgType::IntRange { .. }, ArgType::Int) => true,
   216	        (ArgType::IntRange { min: a, max: b }, ArgType::IntRange { min: x, max: y }) => {
   217	            x <= a && b <= y
   218	        }
   219	        _ => false,
   220	    }
   221	}
   222	fn word(name: &str) -> bool {
   223	    !name.is_empty() && name.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
   224	}
   225	fn offset(text: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> usize {
   226	    let start = text
   227	        .split_inclusive('\n')
   228	        .take(line.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
   229	        .map(str::len)
   230	        .sum::<usize>();
   231	    start
   232	        + text[start..]
   233	            .chars()
   234	            .take(col.saturating_sub(1) as usize)
   235	            .map(char::len_utf8)
   236	            .sum::<usize>()
   237	}
   238	fn declaration(text: &str, line: u32) -> (usize, usize) {
   239	    let start = offset(text, line, 1);
   240	    let end = text
   241	        .split_inclusive('\n')
   242	        .enumerate()
   243	        .skip(line as usize)
   244	        .find(|(_, s)| {
   245	            !s.trim().is_empty() && !s.starts_with(char::is_whitespace) && !s.starts_with("--")
   246	        })
   247	        .map(|(i, _)| offset(text, i as u32 + 1, 1))
   248	        .unwrap_or(text.len());
   249	    (start, end)
   250	}
   251	fn remap_type(kind: &mut ast::ScalarType, names: &BTreeMap<String, String>) {
   252	    match &mut kind.kind {
   253	        TypeKind::Ref { table } => {
   254	            if let Some(name) = names.get(table) {
   255	                *table = name.clone()
   256	            }
   257	        }
   258	        TypeKind::List { item, .. } => remap_type(item, names),
   259	        _ => (),
   260	    }
   261	}
   262	// Closed, capture-avoiding alpha conversion of a single source module. Complex
   263	// declaration families require an explicitly authored compatible source.
   264	struct MappingError {reason:String,unsupported:bool}
   265	impl From<String> for MappingError {
   266	    fn from(reason:String)->Self{Self{reason,unsupported:false}}
   267	}
   268	impl From<&str> for MappingError {
   269	    fn from(reason:&str)->Self{reason.to_owned().into()}
   270	}
   271	fn alpha(target: &[Source], current: &[Source]) -> Result<Option<Vec<Source>>, MappingError> {
   272	    if target.len() != 1 || current.len() != 1 {
   273	        return Ok(None);
   274	    }
   275	    let old = parser::parse_document(&target[0].0, &target[0].1).0;
   276	    let (file, text) = &current[0];
   277	    let doc = parser::parse_document(file, text).0;
   278	    if old.tables.iter().chain(&doc.tables).any(|t| !word(&t.name)) {
   279	        return Ok(None);
   280	    }
   281	    let mut names = BTreeMap::new();
   282	    for next in &doc.tables {
   283	        if old.tables.iter().any(|t| t.name == next.name) {
   284	            continue;
   285	        }
   286	        if let Some(prior) = old.tables.iter().find(|t| {
   287	            next.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&t.name)
   288	                || t.renamed_from.as_ref() == Some(&next.name)
   289	        }) {
   290	            names.insert(next.name.clone(), prior.name.clone());
   291	        }
   292	    }
   293	    if names.is_empty() {
   294	        return Ok(None);
   295	    }
   296	    // A legal table name may also spell a primitive type. Reprinting that
   297	    // identity in an argument/column must never turn a nominal Ref into Int,
   298	    // Principal, etc. Validate the parser's actual type interpretation.
   299	    for name in names.values() {
   300	        let probe = format!("table __rollback_type_probe:\n  value: {name}\n");
   301	        let (parsed, diagnostics) = parser::parse_document("rollback-type-probe.q", &probe);
   302	        if !diagnostics.is_empty()
   303	            || !matches!(parsed.tables.first().and_then(|t|t.columns.first()).map(|c|&c.type_.kind),Some(TypeKind::Ref{table}) if table==name)
   304	        {
   305	            return Err(
   306	                "a table identity spelling reparses as a scalar or unsupported reference type"
   307	                    .into(),
   308	            );
   309	        }
   310	    }
   311	    let outputs = doc
   312	        .tables
   313	        .iter()
   314	        .map(|t| names.get(&t.name).unwrap_or(&t.name))
   315	        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   316	    if outputs.len() != doc.tables.len() || names.keys().any(|name| outputs.contains(name)) {
   317	        return Err(
   318	            "the declared table mapping is ambiguous or captures a current table name".into(),
   319	        );
   320	    }
   321	    let reserved = names
   322	        .keys()
   323	        .chain(names.values())
   324	        .cloned()
   325	        .collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   326	    let mut capture = doc.shapes.iter().any(|s| reserved.contains(&s.name));
   327	    capture |= doc.tables.iter().any(|t| {
   328	        t.rules
   329	            .iter()
   330	            .chain(t.column_reads.iter().map(|(_, r)| r))
   331	            .any(|r| r.bindings.iter().any(|n| reserved.contains(n)))
   332	    });
   333	    fn bindings(body: &[ast::Stmt], reserved: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
   334	        body.iter().any(|s| match s {
   335	            ast::Stmt::Bind { names, .. } => names.iter().any(|n| reserved.contains(n)),
   336	            ast::Stmt::For { name, body, .. } => {
   337	                reserved.contains(name) || bindings(body, reserved)
   338	            }
   339	            ast::Stmt::If {
   340	                then_body,
   341	                else_body,
   342	                ..
   343	            } => bindings(then_body, reserved) || bindings(else_body, reserved),
   344	            ast::Stmt::As { body, .. } => bindings(body, reserved),
   345	            _ => false,
   346	        })
   347	    }
   348	    capture |= doc
   349	        .programs
   350	        .iter()
   351	        .any(|p| bindings(&p.prelude, &reserved) || bindings(&p.body, &reserved));
   352	    capture |= doc
   353	        .functions
   354	        .iter()
   355	        .any(|f| matches!(&f.body,ast::FunctionBody::Block(body) if bindings(body,&reserved)));
   356	    for args in doc
   357	        .programs
   358	        .iter()
   359	        .map(|p| &p.args)
   360	        .chain(doc.functions.iter().map(|p| &p.args))
   361	        .chain(doc.actions.iter().map(|p| &p.args))
   362	    {
   363	        capture |= args.iter().any(|a| reserved.contains(&a.name));
   364	    }
   365	    let mut inspect = doc.clone();
   366	    let mut unsupported = false;
   367	    let mut check = |e: &mut ast::Expr| match e {
   368	        ast::Expr::Name { name, .. } => capture |= reserved.contains(name),
   369	        ast::Expr::Walk { .. } | ast::Expr::Merge(_) => unsupported = true,
   370	        _ => (),
   371	    };
   372	    crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut inspect, &mut check);
   373	    for inv in &mut inspect.invariants {
   374	        crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut inv.value, &mut check);
   375	    }
   376	    for table in &mut inspect.tables {
   377	        for rule in table
   378	            .rules
   379	            .iter_mut()
   380	            .chain(table.column_reads.iter_mut().map(|(_, r)| r))
   381	        {
   382	            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut rule.value, &mut check);
   383	        }
   384	        for column in &mut table.columns {
   385	            for value in [&mut column.read, &mut column.default]
   386	                .into_iter()
   387	                .flatten()
   388	            {
   389	                crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value, &mut check);
   390	            }
   391	        }
   392	        for (value, _) in &mut table.final_exists {
   393	            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(value, &mut check);
   394	        }
   395	    }
   396	    if unsupported {
   397	        return Err(
   398	            "a recursive or merge expression needs an authored compatible table mapping".into(),
   399	        );
   400	    }
   401	    if capture {
   402	        return Err("a table identity mapping captures a local, argument or shape name".into());
   403	    }
   404	    if !doc.actions.is_empty()
   405	        || !doc.effects.is_empty()
   406	        || !doc.maintains.is_empty()
   407	        || !doc.timelines.is_empty()
   408	        || !doc.views.is_empty()
   409	        || !doc.feed_views.is_empty()
   410	        || !doc.backfills.is_empty()
   411	        || !doc.downfills.is_empty()
   412	        || !doc.extractions.is_empty()
   413	        || doc.programs.iter().any(|p| p.delivery.is_some())
   414	        || doc
   415	            .tables
   416	            .iter()
   417	            .any(|t| t.receipts.is_some() || t.columns.iter().any(|c| c.maintained.is_some()))
   418	    {
   419	        return Err(MappingError{reason:"this table identity mapping includes a derived, receipt or migration declaration outside the closed current-source adapter".into(),unsupported:true});
   420	    }
   421	    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, text);
   422	    let mut edits = Vec::<(usize, usize, String)>::new();
   423	    for table in &doc.tables {
   424	        let mut source = super::release_bridge::retained_declaration(file, text, table, &names)?;
   425	        if let Some(name) = names.get(&table.name) {
   426	            let prior = old
   427	                .tables
   428	                .iter()
   429	                .find(|t| &t.name == name)
   430	                .ok_or("missing predecessor identity")?;
   431	            source = header(
   432	                file,
   433	                &source,
   434	                &table.name,
   435	                name,
   436	                prior.renamed_from.as_deref(),
   437	            )?;
   438	        }
   439	        let (a, b) = declaration(text, table.site.line);
   440	        edits.push((a, b, source));
   441	    }
   442	    for inv in &doc.invariants {
   443	        let table = names.get(&inv.table).unwrap_or(&inv.table);
   444	        let mut value = inv.value.clone();
   445	        crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |e| match e {
   446	            ast::Expr::Read(r)
   447	            | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
   448	            | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => {
   449	                if let Some(name) = names.get(&r.table) {
   450	                    r.table = name.clone()
   451	                }
   452	            }
   453	            ast::Expr::Write { table, .. } => {
   454	                if let Some(name) = names.get(table) {
   455	                    *table = name.clone()
   456	                }
   457	            }
   458	            _ => (),
   459	        });
   460	        let target = if let Some(column) = &inv.column {
   461	            format!("{table}.{column} {} only", inv.target)
   462	        } else if inv.target == "always" {
   463	            format!("{table} always")
   464	        } else {
   465	            format!("{table}.{} only", inv.target)
   466	        };
   467	        let (a, b) = declaration(text, inv.site.line);
   468	        edits.push((
   469	            a,
   470	            b,
   471	            format!("invariant {target} {}\n", print::expression(&value)),
   472	        ));
   473	    }
   474	    let mut other = doc.clone();
   475	    other.tables.clear();
   476	    other.invariants.clear();
   477	    let mut reference_error = None;
   478	    crate::bind::intent_walk::document(&mut other, &mut |e| {
   479	        let (table, site) = match e {
   480	            ast::Expr::Read(r)
   481	            | ast::Expr::Exists { read: r, .. }
   482	            | ast::Expr::Created { read: r, .. } => (&r.table, &r.site),
   483	            ast::Expr::Write { table, site, .. } => (&*table, &*site),
   484	            _ => return,
   485	        };
   486	        let Some(name) = names.get(table) else { return };
   487	        if let Some(token) = tokens.iter().find(|t| {
   488	            t.site.line == site.line
   489	                && t.site.col >= site.col
   490	                && t.kind == Kind::Word
   491	                && t.value == *table
   492	        }) {
   493	            let a = offset(text, token.site.line, token.site.col);
   494	            edits.push((a, a + token.value.len(), name.clone()));
   495	        } else {
   496	            reference_error = Some("a table reference has no unambiguous source span".to_owned());
   497	        }
   498	    });
   499	    if let Some(error) = reference_error {
   500	        return Err(error.into());
   501	    }
   502	    for arg in doc
   503	        .programs
   504	        .iter()
   505	        .flat_map(|p| &p.args)
   506	        .chain(doc.functions.iter().flat_map(|p| &p.args))
   507	        .chain(doc.actions.iter().flat_map(|p| &p.args))
   508	        .chain(doc.actions.iter().flat_map(|p| p.returns.iter().flatten()))
   509	    {
   510	        let mut kind = arg.type_.clone();
   511	        remap_type(&mut kind, &names);
   512	        if kind != arg.type_ {
   513	            let (a, b) = arg_type(text, &tokens, arg)?;
   514	            edits.push((a, b, print::scalar_type(&kind)));
   515	        }
   516	    }
   517	    edits.sort_by_key(|(a, b, _)| (*a, *b));
   518	    edits.dedup();
   519	    if edits.iter().any(|(a, b, _)| {
   520	        a > b || *b > text.len() || !text.is_char_boundary(*a) || !text.is_char_boundary(*b)
   521	    }) {
   522	        return Err("an unsupported source span needs an authored compatible mapping".into());
   523	    }
   524	    if edits.windows(2).any(|p| p[0].1 > p[1].0) {
   525	        return Err("overlapping source spans in table identity mapping".into());
   526	    }
   527	    let mut result = text.clone();
   528	    for (a, b, value) in edits.into_iter().rev() {
   529	        result.replace_range(a..b, &value);
   530	    }
   531	    Ok(Some(vec![(file.clone(), result)]))
   532	}
   533	fn header(
   534	    file: &str,
   535	    source: &str,
   536	    from: &str,
   537	    to: &str,
   538	    prior: Option<&str>,
   539	) -> Result<String, String> {
   540	    if prior.is_some_and(|p| !word(p)) {
   541	        return Err("qualified predecessor table origin needs an authored source".into());
   542	    }
   543	    let line = source.lines().next().ok_or("missing table header")?;
   544	    let (tokens, _) = lexer::lex(file, line);
   545	    let name = tokens
   546	        .get(1)
   547	        .filter(|t| t.kind == Kind::Word && t.value == from)
   548	        .ok_or("missing table name span")?;
   549	    let mut edits = vec![(
   550	        offset(line, name.site.line, name.site.col),
   551	        offset(line, name.site.line, name.site.col) + from.len(),
   552	        to.to_owned(),
   553	    )];
   554	    if let Some(at) = tokens.iter().enumerate().skip(2).find_map(|(i,t)| (t.value=="renamed").then_some(i)) {
   555	        let last = tokens
   556	            .get(at + 2)
   557	            .filter(|_| tokens.get(at + 1).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "from"))
   558	            .ok_or("invalid table origin span")?;
   559	        edits.push((
   560	            offset(line, tokens[at].site.line, tokens[at].site.col),
   561	            offset(line, last.site.line, last.site.col) + last.value.len(),
   562	            prior
   563	                .map(|p| format!("renamed from {p}"))
   564	                .unwrap_or_default(),
   565	        ));
   566	    } else if let Some(prior) = prior {
   567	        let colon = tokens
   568	            .iter()
   569	            .find(|t| t.value == ":")
   570	            .ok_or("missing table header colon")?;
   571	        let a = offset(line, colon.site.line, colon.site.col);
   572	        edits.push((a, a, format!(" renamed from {prior}")));
   573	    }
   574	    let mut out = source.to_owned();
   575	    edits.sort_by_key(|(a, _, _)| std::cmp::Reverse(*a));
   576	    for (a, b, value) in edits {
   577	        out.replace_range(a..b, &value);
   578	    }
   579	    Ok(out)
   580	}
   581	pub(super) fn arg_type(
   582	    text: &str,
   583	    tokens: &[lexer::Token],
   584	    arg: &ast::ArgDecl,
   585	) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
   586	    let at = tokens
   587	        .iter()
   588	        .position(|t| t.site == arg.site)
   589	        .ok_or("missing argument source span")?;
   590	    let colon = (at..tokens.len())
   591	        .find(|i| tokens[*i].value == ":")
   592	        .ok_or("missing argument type colon")?;
   593	    let start = tokens.get(colon + 1).ok_or("missing argument type")?;
   594	    let mut depth = 0_i32;
   595	    for (ordinal, t) in tokens.iter().skip(colon + 1).enumerate() {
   596	        // `alias` and `default` are legal first nominal type tokens too.
   597	        if ordinal > 0
   598	            && depth == 0
   599	            && (matches!(
   600	                t.value.as_str(),
   601	                "," | ")" | "=" | "default" | "alias" | "renamed" | "deprecated"
   602	            ) || t.kind == Kind::Newline)
   603	        {
   604	            return Ok((
   605	                offset(text, start.site.line, start.site.col),
   606	                text[..offset(text, t.site.line, t.site.col)]
   607	                    .trim_end()
   608	                    .len(),
   609	            ));
   610	        }
   611	        match t.value.as_str() {
   612	            "(" | "[" | "<" => depth += 1,
   613	            ")" | "]" | ">" => depth -= 1,
   614	            _ => (),
   615	        }
   616	    }
   617	    Err("unterminated argument type source span".into())
   618	}
            .chain(read.seeks.iter_mut().map(|seek| &mut seek.column)) {
            *column = row_column(table, column).into();
        }
        if super::validate_key_names(read).is_err() { return; }
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — normalized named search orders keep their
        // explicit choice for a compatible browse plan; words use normal planning.
        let preferred = read.index.as_ref().filter(|name| !read.order_keys.is_empty()
            && table.indexes.iter().any(|index| &index.name == *name && index.unique && index.is_plain())).cloned();
        if preferred.is_some() { read.index = None; }
        if read.index.is_some() {
            if !rule { self.selected_prefix(table, read); }
            return;
        }
        let named = !read.keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — exact named unique keys select points;
        // binding requires non-null arguments on nullable key columns.
        let covered = named && table.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.unique && index.is_plain()
            && (index.columns.len() == read.keys.len() || table.total_unique(index))
            && index.columns.iter().all(|column| read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column))));
        if rule {
            if named && !covered {
                read.index = Some(self.index(table, read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone()).collect(), None, &read.site, true));
            }
            return;
        }
        if (covered || table.maintained.is_some() && named && table.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.unique && index.columns.iter().all(|column| read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column))))) && read.range.is_none() && read.filter.is_none() && read.matching.is_none() && read.order_keys.is_empty() { return; }
        // Positional keys retain the binder's point/refusal path. They are never
        // silently turned into a predicate on an inferred parent column.

MATERIAL — equivalent unique keys are compared before column identity alignment in [release_bridge_rules.rs:99](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge_rules.rs:99), deleting an explicitly referenced historical index.

Concrete counterexample, v1:

```quarry
table items:
  code: text <=20
  n: int
  unique byCode: code
  public
  online only
query legacyLookup(code: text <=20):
  return items[code] by byCode
```

For v2:

- Change `code` to `label: text <=20 renamed from code`.
- Change the key to `unique byCurrentCode: label`.
- Widen `n` to `int ?`.
- Remove `legacyLookup`.

Static tracing shows `loses_unique` compares `["code"]` against `["label"]`, incorrectly declares uniqueness relaxed, and removes `byCode`. Transport then correctly inserts `byCurrentCode: code`, but the retained legacy query still explicitly requires `byCode`, so the generated view fails compilation.

Neither current-source fallback recovers: both require the missing predecessor query ([release_bridge_current.rs:135](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/release_bridge_current.rs:135)). Deployment therefore refuses an ordinary evolution despite unchanged uniqueness and authority.

Compare keys under the declared injective identities before deciding uniqueness was lost, preserving the explicitly used historical name when its enforcing meaning remains current.

This is a source-derived counterexample; no builds, tests, edits, or review files were produced.
